JWT Middleware — Explained Like a Child 🧠
First: What Problem Does JWT Solve?
Imagine this problem 👇

A user logs in
Then clicks Attendance, Leave, Salary
❓ How does the system know:

This is the same person
They are allowed to see this page
They are not a stranger

👉 JWT solves this.

What Is JWT? (Very Simple)

JWT = Digital ID Card
When a user logs in:

System gives them a secret ID card
Every time they talk to the system, they must show this card
If card is missing or fake → ❌ blocked

This ID card is called a JWT Token.

Where JWT Middleware Fits (Big Picture)
pgsql

USER
│
│  (Request)
▼
JWT MIDDLEWARE  ← 🛑 CHECKPOINT

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 11/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

│
│  (Allowed?)
▼
API ROUTE (Attendance / Leave / Payroll)
│
▼
DATABASE Copy code

So EVERY protected route passes through JWT Middleware first.

What Is “JWT Middleware”?

Middleware = Security Guard 🚔
A middleware is like a security guard at the door.

It asks:

1. Do you have a token?
2. Is it valid?
3. Is it expired?
4. Does your role allow this action?

Only if all answers are ✅ → you can enter.

Step-by-Step: What Happens When User Logs In

Step 1️⃣ User Logs In
Enters email + password
Backend checks database

If correct → ✅

Step 2️⃣ Backend Creates JWT Token
Token contains:

user_id
role (Employee / HR / Admin)
expiry time (e.g. 24 hours)

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 12/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

📦 This token is signed (cannot be edited)

Step 3️⃣ Token Sent to User
Stored in browser (localStorage or cookie)
User doesn’t see details
Browser sends it automatically

Step-by-Step: What Happens on Every Request
Example: User clicks “My Attendance”

Step 1️⃣ Browser Sends Request
It sends:

makefile

Authorization: Bearer <JWT_TOKEN>
Copy code

Step 2️⃣ JWT Middleware Runs 🛑
Middleware checks:

❓ Token exists?
❓ Token valid?
❓ Token expired?
❓ Signature correct?

If ANY answer is NO → ❌ 401 Unauthorized

Step 3️⃣ Role & Permission Check
Middleware checks:

Is role allowed to access attendance?

Examples:
Employee → own attendance ✅
HR → all attendance ✅

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 13/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

Random user → ❌

Step 4️⃣ Route Executes
Only now:

Attendance data fetched
Response sent

What “Protect Routes” Means (Very Clear)

❌ WITHOUT JWT
Anyone can call:

bash

/api/attendance
/api/payroll

Copy code

✅ WITH JWT
Only users who:

Logged in
Have valid token
Have correct role

Can access those routes.

Final Result (Your Question)
Result: Users can now login! ???

✅ YES — AND MORE THAN THAT
After JWT Middleware:

✅ Users can login
✅ Users stay logged in
✅ Unauthorized users are blocked
✅ HR-only pages are protected

🔐 JWT MIDDLEWARE (SECURITY CORE)
JWT = Digital ID Card

How it works:
1. User logs in → JWT issued
2. Every request sends token
3. Middleware checks:

Token exists
Token valid
Token not expired
Role has permission

4. Only then → API executes

Result:
Only authenticated users access system
Unauthorized access blocked
Roles enforced everywhere

🗄️ DATABASE STRATEGY
SQL database (normalized, indexed)

STEP 6️⃣ — FULL UPDATED CODE (IMPORTANT)
Now we will update app.py .
👉 Delete everything inside app.py
👉 Paste the FULL code below

📄 app.py  (FULL CODE — COPY EXACTLY)

python

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from flask_bcrypt import Bcrypt

app = Flask(__name__)

# DATABASE CONFIGURATION
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)

# -------------------
# DATABASE MODEL
# -------------------
class User(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 75/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

# -------------------
# ROUTES
# -------------------

@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "HRMS Day 3: Login system running 🚀"

# REGISTER USER
@app.route("/register", methods=["POST"])
def register():
    data = request.json
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"message": "Email and password required"}), 400

    hashed_password = bcrypt.generate_password_hash(password).decode("utf-8")

    user = User(email=email, password=hashed_password)
    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered successfully"}), 201

# LOGIN USER
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.json
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    user = User.query.filter_by(email=email).first()

    if user and bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, password):
        return jsonify({"message": "Login successful"}), 200
    else:
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid email or password"}), 401

Copy code

if __name__ == "__main__":

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from flask_bcrypt import Bcrypt
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    JWTManager,
    create_access_token,
    jwt_required,
    get_jwt_identity
)

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 83/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

app = Flask(__name__)

# -------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# -------------------

app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "super-secret-key-change-this"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# -------------------
# DATABASE MODEL
# -------------------

class User(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)

# -------------------
# ROUTES
# -------------------

@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "HRMS Day 4: JWT Authentication running 🔐"

# REGISTER
@app.route("/register", methods=["POST"])
def register():
    data = request.json
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"message": "Email and password required"}), 400

    existing_user = User.query.filter_by(email=email).first()

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 84/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

    if existing_user:
        return jsonify({"message": "User already exists"}), 409

    hashed_password = bcrypt.generate_password_hash(password).decode("utf-8")

    user = User(email=email, password=hashed_password)
    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered successfully"}), 201

# LOGIN (RETURNS JWT)
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.json
    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    user = User.query.filter_by(email=email).first()

    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, password):
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid email or password"}), 401

    access_token = create_access_token(identity=user.id)

    return jsonify({
        "message": "Login successful",
        "access_token": access_token
    }), 200

# PROTECTED ROUTE
@app.route("/protected", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def protected():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    return jsonify({
        "message": "You are authorized 🎉",
        "user_id": user_id
    }), 200

Copy code

10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

Perfect screenshot 👍
This is NOT your mistake.
This is a browser security rule that appeared because of where you opened the Console
from.

I’ll explain why it happened, then give you 2 correct ways to fix it — step by step, like a
teacher.

🧠 WHY THIS ERROR IS HAPPENING (VERY SIMPLE)

What the error says (in simple words)
❌ “You are trying to call http://127.0.0.1:5000/register
from a page that is NOT allowed to talk to it.”

This is called Content Security Policy (CSP).

🔴 THE REAL REASON (IMPORTANT)
You opened Developer Console from a Chrome internal page, NOT from your Flask app
page.

Examples of wrong pages:

chrome://extensions

about:blank

New tab page
Any random website

Those pages BLOCK network calls to localhost .

That’s why this error appeared.

🔑 THE GOLDEN RULE (LOCK THIS)
⚠️ You must always open the Console FROM the Flask page itself

Not from anywhere else.

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 103/420

🧠 WHAT THE ERROR ACTUALLY MEANS (VERY SIMPLE)
Key line from the error (THIS IS THE TRUTH):

rust

Table 'hrms_db.user' doesn't exist
Copy code

Translation (plain English):
Your Flask code is trying to use a table named user,
but MySQL does not have that table yet.

So:
❌ Login code is NOT wrong
❌ JWT code is NOT wrong
❌ Browser is NOT wrong
✅ Only the database table was never created

🔴 WHY THIS HAPPENED (IMPORTANT LESSON)
Flask does NOT automatically create tables just because you wrote a model.

Tables are created ONLY when you explicitly run:

python

db.create_all()
Copy code

At some point:
Either this step was skipped
Or it ran before the model existed
Or it ran with app not fully loaded

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify

from flask_jwt_extended import (
    JWTManager,
    create_access_token,
    jwt_required,

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 131/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

    get_jwt_identity

app = Flask(__name__)

# ---------------- CONFIG ----------------
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "change-this-secret"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ---------------- MODEL ----------------
class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "user"   # force table name
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)

# ---------------- ROUTES ----------------
@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "HRMS Day 4: JWT Authentication running 🔐"

@app.route("/register", methods=["POST"])
def register():
    data = request.get_json()

    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"message": "Email & password required"}), 400

    existing_user = User.query.filter_by(email=email).first()
    if existing_user:
        return jsonify({"message": "User already exists"}), 409

    hashed_password = bcrypt.generate_password_hash(password).decode("utf-8")
    user = User(email=email, password=hashed_password)

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 132/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered successfully"}), 201

@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json()

    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")

    user = User.query.filter_by(email=email).first()

    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, password):
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(identity=user.id)

    return jsonify({
        "message": "Login successful",
        "access_token": token
    }), 200

@app.route("/protected", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def protected():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    return jsonify({
        "message": "You are authorized 🎉",
        "user_id": user_id
    })

# -------- CREATE TABLES SAFELY --------
with app.app_context():
    db.create_all()

# ---------------- RUN ----------------
if __name__ == "__main__": Copy code
    app.run(debug=True)

python

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from flask_bcrypt import Bcrypt
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    JWTManager,
    create_access_token,
    jwt_required,
    get_jwt
)

app = Flask(__name__)

# ---------------- CONFIG ----------------
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "change-this-secret"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 141/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ---------------- MODELS ----------------
class Role(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)

class User(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("role.id"))

    role = db.relationship("Role")

# ---------------- ROUTES ----------------
@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "HRMS Day 5: Roles & Permissions running 🧩"

@app.route("/register", methods=["POST"])
def register():
    data = request.get_json()

    email = data["email"]
    password = data["password"]
    role_name = data.get("role", "Employee")

    role = Role.query.filter_by(name=role_name).first()
    if not role:
        role = Role(name=role_name)
        db.session.add(role)
        db.session.commit()

    hashed = bcrypt.generate_password_hash(password).decode("utf-8")
    user = User(email=email, password=hashed, role=role)

    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered with role"}), 201

@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json()
    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first()

    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, data["password"]
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=user.id,
        additional_claims={"role": user.role.name}
    )

    return jsonify({"access_token": token}), 200

@app.route("/employee-only")
@jwt_required()
def employee_only():
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Employee":
        return jsonify({"message": "Access denied"}), 403
    return jsonify({"message": "Employee data visible"}), 200

@app.route("/admin-only")
@jwt_required()
def admin_only():
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403
    return jsonify({"message": "Admin data visible"}), 200

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from flask_bcrypt import Bcrypt
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    JWTManager,
    create_access_token,
    jwt_required,
    get_jwt

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 154/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

)

app = Flask(__name__)

# ================= CONFIG =================
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "super-secret-key"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ================= MODELS =================
class Role(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "roles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)

class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("roles.id"))

    role = db.relationship("Role")

# ================= ROUTES =================
@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "HRMS Auth + Roles running ✅"

@app.route("/register", methods=["POST"])
def register():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)

    email = data.get("email")
    password = data.get("password")
    role_name = data.get("role", "Employee")

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({"message": "Email & password required"}), 400

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 155/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

    role = Role.query.filter_by(name=role_name).first()
    if not role:
        role = Role(name=role_name)
        db.session.add(role)
        db.session.commit()

    if User.query.filter_by(email=email).first():
        return jsonify({"message": "User already exists"}), 409

    hashed = bcrypt.generate_password_hash(password).decode("utf-8")
    user = User(email=email, password=hashed, role=role)

    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered successfully"}), 201

@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)

    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data.get("email")).first()
    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, data.get("passwo
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=user.id,
        additional_claims={"role": user.role.name}
    )

    return jsonify({"access_token": token}), 200

@app.route("/admin-only")
@jwt_required()
def admin_only():
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403
    return jsonify({"message": "Welcome Admin"}), 200

# ================= CREATE TABLES =================
with app.app_context():

# ================= CONFIG =================
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "super-secret-key"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)

# ================= MODELS =================
class Role(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "roles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)

class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("roles.id"))

    role = db.relationship("Role")

class Employee(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "employees"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    full_name = db.Column(db.String(100), nullable=False)
    phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    department = db.Column(db.String(50))
    designation = db.Column(db.String(50))
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"), unique=True)

    user = db.relationship("User")

def register():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)

    role_name = data.get("role", "Employee")
    role = Role.query.filter_by(name=role_name).first()
    if not role:
        role = Role(name=role_name)
        db.session.add(role)
        db.session.commit()

    if User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first():
        return jsonify({"message": "User exists"}), 409

    hashed = bcrypt.generate_password_hash(data["password"]).decode("utf-8")
    user = User(email=data["email"], password=hashed, role=role)

    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered"}), 201

# -------- LOGIN --------
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first()

    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, data["password"]
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=user.id,
        additional_claims={"role": user.role.name}
    )

    return jsonify({"access_token": token}), 200

@app.route("/employee/profile", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def view_own_profile():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    emp = Employee.query.filter_by(user_id=user_id).first()

    if not emp:
        return jsonify({"message": "Profile not found"}), 404

    return jsonify({
        "full_name": emp.full_name,
        "phone": emp.phone,
        "department": emp.department,
        "designation": emp.designation
    })

🔴 WHAT THIS ERROR MEANS (VERY SIMPLE)

Error you see:

css

422 UNPROCESSABLE ENTITY
{ msg: "Subject must be a string" }

Copy code

Plain English:
JWT does not accept numbers as the user identity.
It only accepts text (string).

Right now your code is doing this internally:

python

create_access_token(identity=user.id)
Copy code

Where:

ini

user.id = 1   (number ❌)
Copy code

JWT expects:

arduino

"1" (text ✅)
Copy code

🧠 WHY JWT REQUIRES STRING (CHILD LEVEL)
Think of JWT like an ID card printer.

It prints text only
Numbers must be converted to text
If you give a number → it rejects it

So this is NOT your fault.
This is a JWT library rule.

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 194/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

Login will work
Employee profile will save
Day 6 will be DONE

from flask_bcrypt import Bcrypt
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    JWTManager,
    create_access_token,
    jwt_required,
    get_jwt_identity,
    get_jwt
)
from datetime import datetime, date

app = Flask(__name__)

app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "super-secret-key"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ================= MODELS =================
class Role(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "roles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)

class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("roles.id"))
    role = db.relationship("Role")

class Employee(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "employees"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    full_name = db.Column(db.String(100), nullable=False)
    phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    department = db.Column(db.String(50))
    designation = db.Column(db.String(50))
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"), unique=True)

class Attendance(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "attendance"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"))
    attendance_date = db.Column(db.Date, default=date.today)
    check_in = db.Column(db.DateTime)
    check_out = db.Column(db.DateTime)

# ================= ROUTES =================

def home():
    return "HRMS Day 7: Attendance System ⏱️"

@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])

def login():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first()

    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, data["password"]
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={"role": user.role.name}
    )
    return jsonify({"access_token": token}), 200

# -------- CHECK IN --------
@app.route("/attendance/check-in", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def check_in():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    today = date.today()

    record = Attendance.query.filter_by(
        user_id=user_id,
        attendance_date=today
    ).first()

    if record and record.check_in:
        return jsonify({"message": "Already checked in"}), 400

    if not record:
        record = Attendance(user_id=user_id)

    record.check_in = datetime.now()
    db.session.add(record)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "Checked in successfully"}), 200

# -------- CHECK OUT --------
@app.route("/attendance/check-out", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def check_out():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    today = date.today()

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 205/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

    record = Attendance.query.filter_by(
        user_id=user_id,
        attendance_date=today
    ).first()

    if not record or not record.check_in:
        return jsonify({"message": "Check-in first"}), 400

    if record.check_out:
        return jsonify({"message": "Already checked out"}), 400

    record.check_out = datetime.now()
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "Checked out successfully"}), 200

# -------- VIEW OWN ATTENDANCE --------
@app.route("/attendance/my", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def my_attendance():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    records = Attendance.query.filter_by(user_id=user_id).all()

    data = []
    for r in records:
        data.append({
            "date": r.attendance_date.isoformat(),
            "check_in": r.check_in.strftime("%H:%M:%S") if r.check_in else None,
            "check_out": r.check_out.strftime("%H:%M:%S") if r.check_out else Non
        })

    return jsonify(data), 200

# -------- ADMIN VIEW ALL --------

    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403

    records = Attendance.query.all()

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from flask_bcrypt import Bcrypt
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    JWTManager,
    create_access_token,
    jwt_required,
    get_jwt_identity,
    get_jwt
)
from datetime import datetime, date

app = Flask(__name__)

# ================= CONFIG =================
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "super-secret-key"

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 213/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ================= MODELS =================
class Role(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "roles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True)

class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255))
    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("roles.id"))
    role = db.relationship("Role")

class Employee(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "employees"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    full_name = db.Column(db.String(100))
    department = db.Column(db.String(50))
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"), unique=True)

class AttendanceDay(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "attendance_days"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"))
    attendance_date = db.Column(db.Date, default=date.today)

class AttendancePunch(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "attendance_punches"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    attendance_day_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("attendance_days.id")
    punch_time = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.now)
    punch_type = db.Column(db.String(10))  # IN / OUT

# ================= ROUTES =================
@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "HRMS Day 7 (Option B): Multi-Punch Attendance ⏱️"

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 214/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

# -------- LOGIN --------
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first()

    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, data["password"]
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={"role": user.role.name}
    )
    return jsonify({"access_token": token}), 200

# -------- PUNCH IN / OUT --------
@app.route("/attendance/punch", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def punch():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    punch_type = data.get("type")  # IN or OUT

    if punch_type not in ["IN", "OUT"]:
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid punch type"}), 400

    today = date.today()

    day = AttendanceDay.query.filter_by(
        user_id=user_id,
        attendance_date=today
    ).first()

    if not day:
        day = AttendanceDay(user_id=user_id)
        db.session.add(day)
        db.session.commit()

    punch = AttendancePunch(
        attendance_day_id=day.id,
        punch_type=punch_type
    )

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 215/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

    db.session.add(punch)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({
        "message": f"Punched {punch_type} successfully",
        "time": punch.punch_time.isoformat()
    }), 200

# -------- VIEW OWN ATTENDANCE --------
@app.route("/attendance/my", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def my_attendance():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    days = AttendanceDay.query.filter_by(user_id=user_id).all()

    result = []
    for d in days:
        punches = AttendancePunch.query.filter_by(
            attendance_day_id=d.id
        ).all()

        result.append({
            "date": d.attendance_date.isoformat(),
            "punches": [
                {
                    "time": p.punch_time.strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
                    "type": p.punch_type
                } for p in punches
            ]
        })

    return jsonify(result), 200

# -------- ADMIN VIEW ALL --------
@app.route("/admin/attendance", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def admin_attendance():
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403

    punches = AttendancePunch.query.all()

    return jsonify([
        {
            "attendance_day_id": p.attendance_day_id,
            "time": p.punch_time.isoformat(),
            "type": p.punch_type
        } for p in punches
    ])

# ================= CREATE TABLES =================
with app.app_context():
    db.create_all()

class LeaveRequest(db.Model):

    start_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    end_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    reason = db.Column(db.String(255))
    status = db.Column(db.String(20), default="PENDING")
    applied_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.now)

        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    leave = LeaveRequest(

# -------- APPROVE / REJECT --------
@app.route("/admin/leave/<int:leave_id>", methods=["PUT"])
@jwt_required()
def update_leave(leave_id):
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403

    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    leave = LeaveRequest.query.get(leave_id)

    if not leave:
        return jsonify({"message": "Leave not found"}), 404

    leave.status = data["status"]
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": f"Leave {leave.status}"}), 200

from datetime import datetime, date

app = Flask(__name__)

# ================= CONFIG =================
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_PASSWORD@local
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "super-secret-key"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
bcrypt = Bcrypt(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ================= MODELS =================
class Role(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "roles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True)

class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255))
    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("roles.id"))
    role = db.relationship("Role")

class LeaveRequest(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "leave_requests"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"))
    start_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    end_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    reason = db.Column(db.String(255))
    status = db.Column(db.String(20), default="PENDING")
    applied_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.now)

# ================= ROUTES =================
@app.route("/")
def home():

    return "HRMS Day 8: Leave Management Running"

# -------- LOGIN --------
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first()

    if not user or not bcrypt.check_password_hash(user.password, data["password"]
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={"role": user.role.name}
    )
    return jsonify({"access_token": token}), 200

# -------- APPLY LEAVE --------
@app.route("/leave/apply", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def apply_leave():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    data = request.get_json(force=True)

    leave = LeaveRequest(
        user_id=user_id,
        start_date=date.fromisoformat(data["start_date"]),
        end_date=date.fromisoformat(data["end_date"]),
        reason=data.get("reason")
    )

    db.session.add(leave)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "Leave applied successfully"}), 201

# -------- VIEW MY LEAVES --------
@app.route("/leave/my", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def my_leaves():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    leaves = LeaveRequest.query.filter_by(user_id=user_id).all()

@app.route("/admin/leaves", methods=["GET"])
@jwt_required()
def admin_leaves():
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403

    leaves = LeaveRequest.query.all()
    return jsonify([
        {
            "id": l.id,
            "user_id": l.user_id,
            "start": l.start_date.isoformat(),
            "end": l.end_date.isoformat(),
            "reason": l.reason,
            "status": l.status
        } for l in leaves
    ])

# -------- APPROVE / REJECT LEAVE --------
@app.route("/admin/leave/<int:leave_id>", methods=["PUT"])
@jwt_required()
def update_leave(leave_id):
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403

    data = request.get_json(force=True)
    leave = LeaveRequest.query.get(leave_id)

    if not leave:
        return jsonify({"message": "Leave not found"}), 404

Database hash ❌ does not match
Flask-Bcrypt  comparison ❌ fails

Login returns 401 Invalid credentials

This is very subtle, and even many developers miss this.

🧠 WHAT “Invalid salt” MEANS (CHILD-LEVEL
EXPLANATION)
Think of a password hash like a lock + key system.

The lock (hash) is created using:
a specific algorithm
a specific salt format

The key (password) must be checked using the same rules

“Invalid salt” means:
❌ The stored password hash is NOT compatible with Flask-Bcrypt

In simple words:

👉 The admin password hash stored in the database is corrupted / incompatible

This confirms what we suspected earlier — 100% certainty now.

🧠 WHY THIS HAPPENED (NO BLAME, JUST FACTS)
This happened because:

The admin password hash was inserted manually
Or generated using a different bcrypt implementation
Or copied from a different environment

Even though it looks like bcrypt:

javascript

$2b$12$....
Copy code

Flask-Bcrypt is very strict about format.

So when it tries to read the “salt” inside the hash → 💥 crash.

That’s why:

First you got 401
Then after regenerating → 500 Invalid salt

This is a classic real-world bug, not beginner stuff.

@app.route("/_debug/create-admin")
def create_admin():
    from models import User, Role  # if models inline, ignore this line

    # check if admin role exists
    role = Role.query.filter_by(name="Admin").first()
    if not role:
        role = Role(name="Admin")
        db.session.add(role)
        db.session.commit()

    # check if admin user exists
    user = User.query.filter_by(email="admin@gmail.com").first()
    if user:
        return "Admin already exists"

    hashed = bcrypt.generate_password_hash("123456").decode("utf-8")
    admin = User(
        email="admin@gmail.com",
        password=hashed,
        role_id=role.id

🟢 STEP 1 — CONFIRM LOGIN CODE (NO GUESSING)

Open app.py  in VS Code
Find your /login  route.

It MUST look exactly like this:

python

from werkzeug.security import generate_password_hash, check_password_hash

@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json(force=True)

    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first()

    if not user or not check_password_hash(user.password, data["password"]):
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={"role": user.role.name}
    )

    return jsonify({"access_token": token}), 200
Copy code

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    JWTManager, create_access_token,
    jwt_required, get_jwt_identity, get_jwt
)
from werkzeug.security import generate_password_hash, check_password_hash
from datetime import datetime, date

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 336/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

# ---------------- APP CONFIG ----------------
app = Flask(__name__)

app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_MYSQL_PASSWORD@
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "hrms-secret-key"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ---------------- MODELS ----------------
class Role(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "roles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True)

class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True)

    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("roles.id"))
    role = db.relationship("Role")

class EmployeeProfile(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "employee_profiles"

    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("users.id"))
    full_name = db.Column(db.String(100))
    phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    department = db.Column(db.String(50))
    designation = db.Column(db.String(50))

class Attendance(db.Model):

@app.route("/register", methods=["POST"])
def register():
    data = request.get_json()
    role = Role.query.filter_by(name=data["role"]).first()
    if not role:
        role = Role(name=data["role"])
        db.session.add(role)
        db.session.commit()

    if User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first():
        return jsonify({"message": "User exists"}), 400

    user = User(
        email=data["email"],
        password=generate_password_hash(data["password"]),
        role_id=role.id
    )
    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered"}), 201

# ---------------- LOGIN ----------------

@app.route("/admin/leave/<int:leave_id>", methods=["PUT"])
@jwt_required()
def approve_leave(leave_id):
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403

    leave = LeaveRequest.query.get(leave_id)
    if not leave:
        return jsonify({"message": "Leave not found"}), 404

    leave.status = request.json["status"]
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": f"Leave {leave.status}"})

from werkzeug.security import generate_password_hash, check_password_hash
from datetime import datetime, date

# ---------------- APP SETUP ----------------
app = Flask(__name__)

app.config["SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI"] = "mysql+pymysql://root:YOUR_MYSQL_PASSWORD@
app.config["SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS"] = False
app.config["JWT_SECRET_KEY"] = "hrms-super-secret"

db = SQLAlchemy(app)
jwt = JWTManager(app)

# ---------------- MODELS ----------------
class Role(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "roles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)

class User(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    email = db.Column(db.String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(255), nullable=False)
    role_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey("roles.id"))
    role = db.relationship("Role")

class EmployeeProfile(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "employee_profiles"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer)
    full_name = db.Column(db.String(100))
    phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    department = db.Column(db.String(50))
    designation = db.Column(db.String(50))

class Attendance(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "attendance"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer)
    punch_time = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 355/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

class LeaveRequest(db.Model):
    __tablename__ = "leave_requests"
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer)
    start_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    end_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    reason = db.Column(db.String(255))
    status = db.Column(db.String(20), default="PENDING")

# ---------------- HOME ----------------
@app.route("/")
def home():
    return "HRMS Day 1–8 (Clean & Stable)"

# ---------------- REGISTER ----------------
@app.route("/register", methods=["POST"])
def register():
    data = request.get_json()

    role = Role.query.filter_by(name=data["role"]).first()
    if not role:
        role = Role(name=data["role"])
        db.session.add(role)
        db.session.commit()

    user = User(
        email=data["email"],
        password=generate_password_hash(data["password"]),
        role_id=role.id
    )
    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "User registered"}), 201

# ---------------- LOGIN ----------------
@app.route("/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json()
    user = User.query.filter_by(email=data["email"]).first()

    if not user or not check_password_hash(user.password, data["password"]):
        return jsonify({"message": "Invalid credentials"}), 401

# ---------------- PROFILE ----------------
@app.route("/employee/profile", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def profile():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    data = request.get_json()

    p = EmployeeProfile(
        user_id=user_id,
        full_name=data["full_name"],
        phone=data["phone"],
        department=data["department"],
        designation=data["designation"]
    )
    db.session.add(p)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "Profile saved"})

# ---------------- ATTENDANCE ----------------
@app.route("/attendance/punch", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def punch():
    db.session.add(Attendance(user_id=get_jwt_identity()))
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Punch recorded"})

# ---------------- LEAVE ----------------
@app.route("/leave/apply", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def apply_leave():
    d = request.get_json()
    leave = LeaveRequest(
        user_id=get_jwt_identity(),
        start_date=date.fromisoformat(d["start_date"]),
        end_date=date.fromisoformat(d["end_date"]),

        reason=d["reason"]
    )
    db.session.add(leave)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Leave applied"})

# ---------------- ADMIN APPROVE ----------------
@app.route("/admin/leave/<int:leave_id>", methods=["PUT"])
@jwt_required()
def approve_leave(leave_id):
    if get_jwt()["role"] != "Admin":
        return jsonify({"message": "Admins only"}), 403

    leave = LeaveRequest.query.get(leave_id)
    leave.status = request.json["status"]
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": f"Leave {leave.status}"})

# ---------------- INIT DB ----------------
with app.app_context():
    db.create_all()

“You cannot delete a parent table while another table depends on it.”

So:
employees  table → depends on users
users  table → depends on roles

That’s why MySQL refuses to drop them.

✅ This is correct behavior
❌ This is not a mistake

🧠 GOLDEN DATABASE RULE (LOCK THIS)
Always drop child tables first, then parent tables

Dependency chain in your DB is:

bash

roles
  ↓
users
  ↓
employees

Copy code

So the correct drop order is:

bash

employees → users → roles

MySQL is correctly saying:

That’s why every time you drop one table, another FK pops up.

🧠 IMPORTANT DATABASE RULE (LOCK THIS)
When a schema is polluted and you want a clean reset,
you do NOT manually chase foreign keys.

Instead, you temporarily disable FK checks, reset, then re-enable them.

This is standard practice.

🧠 PAYROLL ENGINE — BIG PICTURE (CHILD LEVEL)

Rows = employees

1️⃣ CLARIFICATION — EMPLOYEE MASTER WITHOUT HISTORY (VERY
IMPORTANT)
You asked a very valid question:

If Employee Master does not store history, how will HR know active/inactive employees,
filtering, searching, etc.?

✅ Correct Answer (Enterprise Practice)
Employee Master DOES store current state, not history.

That means it CAN and SHOULD store:

Current Status (Active / Inactive / Resigned / Terminated / Hold)
Current Grade
Current Basic
Current Role
Current Department
Current Payroll Flags

❌ It should NOT store:
Old grades
Old basics
Old status changes
Increment history

🔁 Where history lives
History lives in event tables:

Increment Form table
Status Change Form table
Promotion Form table
Resignation Form table

🧠 How HR works in real systems
https://chatgpt.com/g/g-p-694bb6cec0308191938e19c47ebc9e0d-hrms/c/694bb911-9100-8324-8496-7890d4877218 413/420



10/02/2026, 14:28 HRMS - HRMS Project Breakdown

HR filters/searches Employee Master → sees current status
HR opens timeline/history view → reads data from form tables

So:
Employee Master = snapshot
Forms = timeline

This design gives you:
Fast filters
Clean payroll
Full audit trail
Zero data corruption

✔ Your requirement for:
Active / inactive count
Filters
Search bar

👉 All of that is perfectly handled by Employee Master.
